Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the apoptosis object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 37. The moduli space has dimension at most 122 with 1 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_37(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 37. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^122(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the apoptosis space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 59 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 972. QED.
